| 08-1692 | Literature and permanent notes differ by degree on a gradient: from a single-source idea that is mostly quoted text, to one with restatement in your own words, to one with commentary and links to your permanent notes, to pure synthesis. | The distinction I make between lit notes and permanent notes is one of degree more than anything else. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/11286#Comment_11286 | davecan | 2021-03-27 | literature-note,own-words | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1693 | Keep literature notes in their original context after making permanent notes from them; a permanent note combines bits from several literature notes, and the remaining bits can feed other notes later. | I don't delete the original literature note because I often use other bits and pieces in yet additional permanent m | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/11320#Comment_11320 | Steve625 | 2021-03-28 | literature-note | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1694 | A source's literature note can mix metadata, a link to the source, all annotations copied out, a list of zettels that arose from it, and a 3-sentence summary; the only difference from a permanent note is a references section. | The only difference between a "literature note" as a ZK note, and a "permanent note" as a ZK is if there's an entry in the References section. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/11321#Comment_11321 | gescho | 2021-03-29 | literature-note,sources | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1695 | Condense a source by progressive summarization (highlight, extract, re-highlight, reorder original text) into a compressed form, then chop that into zettels (citing Forte). | This week, I've condensed a 140-page book down to 2300 words, just by highlighting and reordering original text. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/11321#Comment_11321 | gescho | 2021-03-29 | literature-note,process | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1696 | Maintain traceability: source note lists its literature notes, each literature note links back to the source note, and each permanent note has a references section linking to the literature or source note. | This is how I maintain traceability from note to source. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/11324#Comment_11324 | davecan | 2021-03-29 | literature-note,sources | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1697 | The format for capturing from a source can vary with the source (a note per idea with its citation, a separate source note linked from idea notes, or a source structure note listing them); what matters is that each has title, idea, and reference. | They all include title, idea, reference. These methods cover the basics. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/13282#Comment_13282 | Will | 2021-10-15 | literature-note,sources | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1698 | A literature note can hold a summary, salient points, and full bibliographic details; other notes cite it briefly (e.g. "Weiser 1996") instead of copying full references into every note. | I don't see the point in copying out full bibliographic details for every note. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/13289#Comment_13289 | rupertius | 2021-10-16 | literature-note,sources | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1699 | Don't equate a source with a topic: one source can address several topics, so ideas from it should be detached from the source to be used in other contexts. | I wouldn't equate the source with the topic either, as a source can address multiple topics | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9312#Comment_9312 | myn_user | 2020-12-04 | literature-note,sources | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1700 | A bibliographic note should contain at minimum a one-sentence summary of the source (which also tests your understanding), its key topics and ideas, related sources and zettels, and your evaluation of it. | A one-sentence summary (to jolt the memory when reviewing. This also tests my understanding of the book as a whole) | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/1867/bibliographic-notes | Sev_L | 2021-06-13 | literature-note,sources,review | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1701 | Name note conventions that emerge from practice rather than imposing categories up front; categories are imposed, conventions are exposed. | categories are imposed, conventions are exposed. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/12206#Comment_12206 | Sev_L | 2021-06-14 | literature-note | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1702 | Note categories add work without benefit; if you can't tell what a note is about from its content, the writing has gone wrong. | And if I can't tell what the note is about from looking at the content, then something has gone wrong in the writing of it. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/12204#Comment_12204 | MartinBB | 2021-06-14 | literature-note,writing-style | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1703 | For a literature review, give each article's note a template with fields for relevant literature and theory, purpose, study design, major findings, and conclusions and limitations, alongside a citation title and a short summary. | In theory, it should be pretty easy to establish a new template where the top part becomes spots for those worksheet areas. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/2369/phd-student-thinking-about-organizing-her-literature-review-via-zettels-thoughts | jeannelking | 2022-08-30 | literature-note,sources | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1704 | A book note can record the author's goal for the book and your own goal in reading it, alongside citation, author information, and your notes. | Authors Goal with the Book, My Goal in Reading the Book | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/16394#Comment_16394 | Nick | 2022-09-01 | literature-note,sources | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1705 | Link each source note to its entry in reference-management software. | linking each source note to an entry in your reference management software is a best practice these days. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/16376#Comment_16376 | Andy | 2022-08-30 | literature-note,sources | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1706 | Separate reading from note-writing: take handwritten work notes while reading (an article at a time, a book a chapter at a time), then write zettels in your own words that pass on your thoughts rather than transcribing information; this speeds reading and keeps the system parsimonious. | emphasizing the criterion of passing my thoughts and contributions on a matter to them and not transcribing information for the mere fact of collecting data. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/18340#Comment_18340 | paribanufreitas | 2023-07-24 | literature-note,process,own-words | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1707 | Write notes in your own words and lean little on direct quotations: a note in your own words plugs into different contexts, while one bound to the source's wording and context is too rigid to link later. | If you can write a note in your own words, you can more easily plug-and-play that note into different contexts | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/12287#Comment_12287 | gralka | 2021-06-25 | own-words,linking,sources | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1708 | Size a note by what will be most usable to your future self given the content's established structure: a whole cake recipe belongs on one card, not split into flour and oven notes. | I guarantee that in her recipe collection any cake recipe went on only one card. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/18637#Comment_18637 | Andy | 2023-09-28 | literature-note,atomicity | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1709 | Keep a recipe-like whole on one zettel, but give related subtopics their own zettels connected to it; how to parse a source is partly your choice. | However, there are many aspects of the cooking that could be on separate cards. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/18644#Comment_18644 | GeoEng51 | 2023-09-29 | literature-note,atomicity | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1710 | Not every bullet in a literature note needs to become a permanent note; most can stay as page references cited from a relevant main note ("For more on X, see Author p. N") (citing Luhmann via Schmidt). | I think there’s an assumption out there that one ought develop every bullet point literature note they take. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/2168/luhmann-s-main-note-to-literature-note-ratio | scottscheper | 2022-03-04 | literature-note,sources | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1711 | The ratio of reading notes to zettels is meaningless to target: some books yield many notes and few zettels, others turn every note into a zettel, and you can't tell in advance. | Determining a ratio of notes to zettel is so variable as to be meaningless. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/14757#Comment_14757 | Will | 2022-03-04 | literature-note,process | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1712 | When turning literature notes into zettels, write each new zettel in direct response to an existing zettel, so you develop fewer topics further instead of scattering (citing Ahrens). | When developing a topic in your Zettelkasten, a new zettel should be in direct response to an existing Zettel | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/2508/zettel-feedback-creating-zettels | Mach3Maelstrom | 2023-02-04 | literature-note,process,linking | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1713 | For "pure utilitarian facts" with nothing to comment on, link them to more general concepts and write a few lines on why the fact mattered to you and how you arrived at it. | What I suspect is worth doing is drawing connections between "pure utilitarian facts" and more general concepts/patterns, as well as summarizing the context in which you found a given thing important to remember. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/6694#Comment_6694 | brennen | 2020-06-13 | own-words,linking | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1714 | Develop your own definition of a concept by collecting other people's definitions as a sequence of notes beneath it and using them to rewrite yours. | Eventually you can say use the 8 different definitions to help inform you in rewriting your own definition of it. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/6759#Comment_6759 | Nick | 2020-06-16 | own-words,sources | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1715 | Keep literature notes separate and visible as a pile that forces you to process them soon; filed in the ZK they lose their context over time until you must reread the source or discard them. | The main problem would be of putting a literature note into the zettelkasten, time passes and you forget the context in which it was written. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/6804#Comment_6804 | Nick | 2020-06-18 | literature-note,failure-mode | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1716 | Write your own concise version of the concept first, then add a short quote only when the author's phrasing is already concise, well written, or appealing; keep a quote worth rereading in the ZK so you need not hunt for it later. | Typically, I write my version of the concept first and then add the quote from the author. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/17204#Comment_17204 | GeoEng51 | 2023-01-03 | own-words,sources | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1717 | Never present a quote naked: introduce it with context, paraphrase, questions about the author's premise, or commentary that aids understanding and connection. | I've learned that I want to "introduce" every quote and not present it naked. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/17207#Comment_17207 | Will | 2023-01-04 | own-words,sources | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1718 | During review, refactor older quote-heavy notes by rephrasing or extracting the ideas and discarding the author's wording, so quotes shrink over time. | With my review circuit, I tend to refactor older notes with quotes, either rephrasing them or extracting the ideas discarding the author's verbalization. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/17207#Comment_17207 | Will | 2023-01-04 | own-words,review | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1719 | Inside each note put one clear claim in your own words, a sentence or two on why it matters for your project, and at least one explicit link saying how it relates (e.g. "supports/challenges X"). | Inside each note, aim for one clear claim in your own words, a sentence or two of why it matters for your project, and at least one explicit link | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24304#Comment_24304 | Lawyer147 | 2025-12-17 | own-words,atomicity,link-context | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1720 | Over-capture small argument steps while exploring, then compress the chain, keeping steps reusable in other arguments and merging ones that only make sense inside one essay. | In the exploration phase it’s fine to over capture; later you can “compress” the chain and keep only those steps you might want to reuse in a different argument | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24304#Comment_24304 | Lawyer147 | 2025-12-17 | process,review | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1721 | Mark literature-type notes with an ID prefix (e.g. n vs z) so they are easy to tell apart and filter; keep linked ones in the ZK as sources for zettels, and delete unlinked, irrelevant ones later. | I may delete unlinked and irrelevant note-style zettels at some point in the future. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/4626#Comment_4626 | rhubarb | 2020-03-09 | literature-note,id | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |
| 08-1722 | When marking up a paper, note which current projects it could serve and which existing zettels you could create or expand from it, so you are writing toward your own zettels before anything enters the ZK. | or on which Zettels I think I can create or expand from it's materials. | https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/4661#Comment_4661 | John | 2020-03-10 | literature-note,process | Vanilla API v2 2026-11-04 |

## Conflicts noticed

- Creating notes on impulse without moving them toward maturity is risky and leads to the Collector's Fallacy (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23959#Comment_23959) vs Only taking notes on ideas you intend to advance on some implicit timeline is dangerous (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23962#Comment_23962)
- A solution to a specific problem warrants its own standalone note (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/26390#Comment_26390) vs Keep a problem and its solution together on one note, citing Sascha (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/26396#Comment_26396)
- Add "solutions" as a seventh building-block type (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/26392#Comment_26392) vs After the learning phase, develop ideas as feels appropriate rather than forcing a predefined block taxonomy (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/26393#Comment_26393)
- Small, precise ideas are more stimulating and atomic ideas more reusable (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23975#Comment_23975) vs Pursuing a very small note scale can be counter-productive and cause analysis paralysis (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/26155#Comment_26155)
- Notes are incomplete without links (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/26149#Comment_26149) vs A card in an outliner needs no explicit link because its outline position links it (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/26162#Comment_26162) (same poster, tomp)
- Ask how granular a note is rather than whether it is atomic (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23803#Comment_23803) vs An atomic note contains exactly one knowledge building block, so atomicity is binary (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23814#Comment_23814)
- Atomicity is not desirable for zettels; cards should be molecules, not atoms (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25172#Comment_25172) vs A good atomic note is exactly one knowledge building block (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23814#Comment_23814)
- A card about a subject such as "Bob" with compound content is wanted (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25199#Comment_25199) vs A bare entity like "Bob" is far too small to be an atomic idea (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25219#Comment_25219)
- Focus on cardcraft and purpose, not whether a note is sufficiently atomic (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25232#Comment_25232) vs Measure atomicity as the percent of notes that are atomic (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25109#Comment_25109)
- Use tags to mark how close a zettel is to atomic, treating atomicity as a goal (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23652#Comment_23652) vs Atomicity is not mandatory for every note; bypass it when it causes difficulty (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23694#Comment_23694)
- Atomic does not mean short; establishing an irreducible relation often needs more writing (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23691#Comment_23691) vs Distilling an idea into a short title statement is deep synthesis, and an outline of titles can suffice without bodies (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23694#Comment_23694)
- Writing a full-fledged note is sometimes a waste; go straight for the prompt (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23740#Comment_23740) vs Keep the deep thinking behind a statement in the note body (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23694#Comment_23694)
- Retain a source snippet as the core of an atomic note (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23736#Comment_23736) vs A short statement is shallow when copied or loosely reformulated from a source (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23694#Comment_23694)
- A system without a recognizable network of notes is not a Zettelkasten (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23865#Comment_23865) vs A Zettelkasten exists when the author thinks with the slip box's support; the box need not express the whole thinking (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23731#Comment_23731)
- Exact quotations, including formal definitions, belong in the literature note (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/3436/definitions-in-literature-note-vs-zettel-note) vs If you need an exact expression such as an equation, include it in the note (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25173#Comment_25173)
- A note need not contain only one atom; a reading note can collect possible atoms (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24974#Comment_24974) vs Keep notes small, each containing just one thought (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24978#Comment_24978)
- With full-text search, note length is practically irrelevant (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24974#Comment_24974) vs Seek tools that keep notes small and focused (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24978#Comment_24978)
- Per-section keywords and comments work in well-structured long notes (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24980#Comment_24980) vs Per-note keywords and comments are only feasible with individual notes (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24978#Comment_24978)
- Bibliographic data at the top of the source file with reading notes of any length below (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24929#Comment_24929) vs Create multiple individual notes for the same source instead of one reading note (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24981#Comment_24981)
- Any note containing a source quotation is a literature note, even with own comments (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/26565#Comment_26565) vs A note may start as a quotation and become a regular note as comments are added (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24981#Comment_24981)
- With a typology, an atomic concept note only defines the thing; otherwise it is another type or not atomic (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25370#Comment_25370) vs What counts as a building block depends on field and the note's purpose (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25376#Comment_25376)
- Write zettels with their local context in mind rather than perfectly self-contained (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25332#Comment_25332) vs A card's subject should be explicit in the card itself (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25326#Comment_25326) (weak)
- The components of a claim (evidence, theory, argument, analysis) should be explicit in the basic note model (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25311#Comment_25311) vs Formal methods are too complex for personal notes; use them only as loose inspiration (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25314#Comment_25314)
- One-sentence notes are dangerous because radically atomic claims lack context (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5751#Comment_5751) vs A single thought is no longer than a sentence or two (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5842#Comment_5842)
- Bare facts do not deserve their own zettel; keep them in reference material or SRS (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5751#Comment_5751) vs For scholarly work, individual facts must be captured in the note system (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5800#Comment_5800)
- Zettels should be no longer than a short paragraph (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5842#Comment_5842) vs Let a note grow to at most around 5-10 paragraphs (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/7215#Comment_7215)
- Capturing into an untitled daily note removes cognitive overhead (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/1072/principle-of-atomicity-file-based-zettelkasten-vs-roam-research) vs Being forced to title a note makes you state its core statement (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5759#Comment_5759)
- Not being able to link to inbox captures right away may be good (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5751#Comment_5751) vs Finer granularity is valuable because you may want to link to a single captured sentence (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/1072/principle-of-atomicity-file-based-zettelkasten-vs-roam-research)
- A person note is atomic with the person as its unit (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24520#Comment_24520) vs Atomic notes are about concepts and ideas, not facts; person facts belong in sources or literature notes (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25194#Comment_25194)
- Person notes are important nodes worth keeping even when empty (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24532#Comment_24532) vs Creating nodes for people and most concepts is busywork (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24544#Comment_24544)
- Whether a note is atomic depends on fitting a self-defined knowledge-atom type (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24526#Comment_24526) vs Atomic notes are about concepts and ideas, not facts (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25194#Comment_25194)
- Keep source info in a reference manager via citekeys, no literature notes in the system (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24526#Comment_24526) vs Store literature notes in the system with a summary on top and highlights below (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24519#Comment_24519)
- Split a solution across cards, with the justifying argument on its own card(s) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/26411#Comment_26411) vs Record every step of a troubleshooting process in one note (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/26425#Comment_26425)
- Notes should be brief; 300 words is too long (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9102#Comment_9102) vs A 300-word note is already sufficiently atomic (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9099#Comment_9099)
- Related paragraphs need not be split; chasing granularity paralyzes (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9089#Comment_9089) vs Split the note into three separate idea notes (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9105#Comment_9105)
- A topic note is more useful holding several distinct ideas (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9162#Comment_9162) vs A definition or one argument is the atomic unit; split definition from value argument (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9147#Comment_9147)
- Title a note with a precise full sentence (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9147#Comment_9147) vs Use ID plus keyword title derived last (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9162#Comment_9162)
- Length doesn't matter, handiness does (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9139#Comment_9139) vs Ideal length is paragraph-to-section; too short is noise (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9171#Comment_9171)
- Gemmell's hold-the-whole-idea test is not universal; over-atomizing gives a choppy ZK (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/17094#Comment_17094) vs Atomization works in programming so should work elsewhere (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/17106#Comment_17106)
- An atomic thought is a subject-predicate declarative sentence (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/17095#Comment_17095) vs Not all logically correct statements fit subject-predicate form (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/17113#Comment_17113)
- Format notes hyper-simply like Wozniak's cards; page-size notes bog down retention (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/1215/i-believe-effective-learning-twenty-rules-of-formulating-knowledge-is-essential-reading) vs Hyper-fragmentation for memory serves a different purpose from a ZK (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/6895#Comment_6895)
- Link basics even with trivial connections to optimize the network (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/1215/i-believe-effective-learning-twenty-rules-of-formulating-knowledge-is-essential-reading) vs A ZK is meant to generate new insight, not aid memorization (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/7806#Comment_7806)
- Prefer a new note over adding to an existing one, since adding may break atomicity (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/11560#Comment_11560) vs When a source revisits a concept, edit the new material into the original note (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/11576#Comment_11576)
- Short connected notes are better than long ones (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/11560#Comment_11560) vs Atomic does not necessarily mean short (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/11576#Comment_11576)
- Each link needs an explanation of why it is linked (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/2251/atomicity-connecting-notes) vs Not every link needs an explanation (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/15411#Comment_15411)
- Keep long source notes inside the Zettelkasten (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25130#Comment_25130) vs The Zettelkasten indexes the long literature note through extracted atomic notes (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25129#Comment_25129) (weak)
- One big note in book-like order is easier to maintain than separate notes plus links (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/1107/folder-vs-summary-atomicity-vs-complete-source-of-truth) vs Atomic notes give more options for exploring and re-contextualizing (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/6054#Comment_6054)
- A comparison of two concepts is many ideas and should be split (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/6020#Comment_6020) vs Longer standalone "molecule" notes are a more complete source (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/6052#Comment_6052)
- Write long notes first and atomize only when needed (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/6055#Comment_6055) vs A topic's parts as headings are not atomic ideas and should be separate notes (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/6020#Comment_6020)
- Titles belong only on structure/topic notes; for thought notes the ID matters more (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/8898#Comment_8898) vs Write long keyword-rich sentence titles because the title is the main entry point (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/8888#Comment_8888)
- Keep filenames under 50 characters with the full title inside the note (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/8905#Comment_8905) vs Long sentence titles with many keywords (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/8888#Comment_8888)
- Keep several ideas together and split one off only when you want to reference it (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9420#Comment_9420) vs Atomic notes each hold one whole discrete idea, built at the atomic level (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9408#Comment_9408)
- Split at every "but/however/although", since smaller notes mean more surface area (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/13941#Comment_13941) vs Write notes as microdumps and split only when more formality is needed (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/13925#Comment_13925)
- Split at every "however" (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/13941#Comment_13941) vs Keep notes "functionally atomic", open to more sources and ideas rather than rigid fragments (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/13946#Comment_13946)
- No direct quotation in zettels, even literature notes, citing Ahrens (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/13929#Comment_13929) vs Zettels flexible enough to add quotes (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/13946#Comment_13946)
- Each note should be based on one concept; two concepts make two notes (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/21261#Comment_21261) vs Personal-history zettels can be longer and hold several knit-together ideas (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/21260#Comment_21260)
- Each note should be based on one concept (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/21261#Comment_21261) vs A large multi-section note is still useful when it represents one whole idea (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/21263#Comment_21263)
- Word count is irrelevant, atomicity is the point (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/13295#Comment_13295) vs A 300-400 word draft signals wordiness; aim shorter (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/1651/how-long-a-zettel-is-too-long)
- Write notes as succinct shorthand lists, not narrative sentences (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25243#Comment_25243) vs Lecture notes can be long single-file texts (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25242#Comment_25242) (weak)
- Don't use the title as a stand-alone summary; put search keywords in it (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/19715#Comment_19715) vs A well-designed title is the best summary of the note (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/19728#Comment_19728)
- Keep titles to about 80 characters or less (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/2839/title-length-sweet-spot) vs There is no rule on title length (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25137#Comment_25137)
- Digital zettels don't need titles (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24806#Comment_24806) vs Give each zettel a good title (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/16337#Comment_16337)
- Put the UUID at the end of the title (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/19719#Comment_19719) vs Put the UUID at the beginning of the file name (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/19735#Comment_19735)
- Link by UID so links survive title changes (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/16878#Comment_16878) vs Linking by file name is better because it works across software (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/20631#Comment_20631)
- Drop UIDs; descriptive titles make self-describing links (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/1039/why-are-uid-necessary-used) vs Don't ditch UIDs (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5679#Comment_5679)
- IDs should only identify notes; Folgezettel is a bad idea digitally (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25193#Comment_25193) vs Folgezettel numbering is a failsafe giving implicit neighbor links (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24082#Comment_24082)
- Title notes as propositions or questions, not concepts or topics (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/20508#Comment_20508) vs A generalized title like "Compelling Protagonist - Bad Decisions" is sufficient (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/22389#Comment_22389)
- Make titles positively worded complete-phrase claims (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/10897#Comment_10897) vs Titles-as-claims cannot be a universal rule; title form depends on note type (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/22488#Comment_22488)
- Write the title last (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/19721#Comment_19721) vs Use each claim as a tentative title first and pare it down to develop the claim (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24511#Comment_24511)
- Write zettels for your future self, not a general readership (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/19710#Comment_19710) vs A good note should be understandable by someone else (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/1345/teach-me-something-new)
- Split a note whose title doesn't summarize everything in it (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/2508/zettel-feedback-creating-zettels) vs Lecture notes can stay long single-file texts without atomizing (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25242#Comment_25242)
- Distill notes succinctly with shorthand and lists (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25243#Comment_25243) vs Idea zettels should capture enough to revive the idea, even at 200-300 words (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/3109/the-length-of-complete-ideas)
- Use links as phrases inside running text (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/20738#Comment_20738) vs Use a SEE ALSO section and footnote links so the body flows without interspersed wikilinks (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/2932/critique-my-note-refactoring-journey)
- Keep tags slightly general so searches surface less-related ideas (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/19231#Comment_19231) vs Split a tag into more specific tags once it covers 5 to 10 zettels (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/19237#Comment_19237)
- Put metadata at the bottom so the note starts with the title (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/2386#Comment_2386) vs Put ID and tags at the top so nothing sits between title and body (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/2419#Comment_2419)
- Person and concept notes are busywork (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24544#Comment_24544) vs Attribute each argument to a person, source, and time via person notes (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24520#Comment_24520)
- Links should be non-directional; make every link both ways (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/8919#Comment_8919) vs Link direction carries meaning; manually placed backlinks are inferior because they erase it (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/8923#Comment_8923)
- Write the note first, unlinked, then look for connections (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/8943#Comment_8943) vs Start most new notes from a link, usually inside a structure note (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/4004#Comment_4004)
- Search existing notes for connections every time you make a new note (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/7282#Comment_7282) vs Don't link while taking notes; leave linking until later if needed (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9703#Comment_9703)
- Search existing notes for connections every time you make a new note (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/7282#Comment_7282) vs Don't force links for orphan notes; let later work decide when and whether a note gets linked (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/4015#Comment_4015)
- Encode link relationship types formally and machine-parsably with the link (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/8894#Comment_8894) vs Standard link-type vocabularies are too limited for most notes (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/8927#Comment_8927)
- Every link should be a qualified relationship conveying type and meaning (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23824#Comment_23824) vs Don't be too specific about the nature of a relationship, since your view of it changes (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25828#Comment_25828)
- Make lots of notes and link them liberally (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/11456#Comment_11456) vs Links should be selected, formatted, and placed intentionally; automatic backlinking causes overlinking (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23832#Comment_23832, https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/19206#Comment_19206)
- Avoid backlinks because they lack link context (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/13007#Comment_13007) vs A bare backlink is sometimes better than no link (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/8900#Comment_8900)
- Use links to subject notes instead of tags for classification (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/19206#Comment_19206) vs Shared hashtags are the best way to reach a thematic cluster (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9687#Comment_9687)
- Don't link from an idea note to a broad topic note; link from the topic note instead (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/12321#Comment_12321) vs Put an up:: [[parent]] link at the top of each note (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23832#Comment_23832)
- A note's title is not the descriptor of the links to it (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/12440#Comment_12440) vs A link whose implied title conveys the relationship needs no added context (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23824#Comment_23824)
- Let structure notes grow big rather than splitting, so entry points aren't lost (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23477#Comment_23477) vs As structure notes grow, split them into a network of smaller structure notes (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23474#Comment_23474)
- Let structure notes grow big rather than splitting (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23477#Comment_23477) vs If a structure note gets too large its purpose is diluted (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23488#Comment_23488)
- Build structure notes bottom-up; top-down structures languish (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/20537#Comment_20537) vs Top-down (outline first) gives more order than bottom-up (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/20530#Comment_20530)
- Don't create structure notes at the start; premature categorization (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5738#Comment_5738) vs Keep an index from the very start and add each new note sequence to it (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5750#Comment_5750)
- Take a ready-made outline/TOC from a source into your note system to get the big picture first (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23031#Comment_23031) vs Skim for an overview but don't outline or summarize a source (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23030#Comment_23030)
- Mark structure notes with a title prefix such as MOC or § (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/9035#Comment_9035) vs No special note type or naming for MOCs; they blend with other notes (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/23160#Comment_23160)
- Keep a single entry-point note from which every note is reachable (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/26688#Comment_26688) vs Past a critical mass, enter the Zettelkasten by targeted search rather than links from structure notes (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/11457#Comment_11457)
- Skip structure zettels; tags are better because a note can carry many (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/8256#Comment_8256) vs Structure notes beat tag lists because they give multiple hand-ordered views (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5728#Comment_5728)
- Every new note must connect to a hub, structure note, or literature list (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/22660#Comment_22660) vs Only create a structure note if and when you want one (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5369#Comment_5369)
- Folgezettel numbers put thinking in a straitjacket in a digital ZK (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/4948#Comment_4948) vs Luhmann IDs force you to read existing zettels to place each new note, so notes integrate better (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5050#Comment_5050)
- Folgezettel are a paper-only feature, not needed digitally (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/8294#Comment_8294) vs Search cannot replace the sequence browsing Folgezettel enables (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/26017#Comment_26017)
- After ~2000 zettels, Luhmann IDs added no value and were harmful friction (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24795#Comment_24795) vs The friction of Folgezettel placement is productive and sparks ideas (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/20333#Comment_20333)
- Luhmann IDs need no maintenance (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/4952#Comment_4952) vs Tracking Folgezettel codes overloads cognition and stalls the practice (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/21710#Comment_21710)
- Have a clear, precise reason why a zettel follows its predecessor (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/17123#Comment_17123) vs A Folgezettel is any order that makes sense to you; no requirement beyond your sense they relate (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/8235#Comment_8235)
- Have a clear, precise reason why a zettel follows its predecessor (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/17123#Comment_17123) vs Place each new card by "best local fit" (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/20318#Comment_20318)
- IDs are unnecessary; descriptive file names suffice (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/832#Comment_832) vs Don't ditch UIDs; title-only systems break (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5679#Comment_5679)
- Drop UIDs from titles; title-only links are self-describing (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/1039/why-are-uid-necessary-used) vs A unique ID gives a stable identifier that doesn't break on rename (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5545#Comment_5545)
- Put the ID in both the file name and the note body (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/5591#Comment_5591) vs Keep the ID separate from the note title and file name (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/236#Comment_236)
- Use an ID of a short subject keyword plus a timestamp (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/20328#Comment_20328) vs Encoding a subject heading in the UID is generally inadvisable (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/19336#Comment_19336)
- Link with a bare wikilink to the UID for maximum compatibility (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24678#Comment_24678) vs Link by file name and keep the UID in YAML (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/22495#Comment_22495)
- Use shorter IDs because long timestamps crowd titles (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/598/zettel-id-format) vs Include seconds in timestamp IDs so no two notes collide (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/1842#Comment_1842)
- A literature note should be in your own words with quotes used very sparingly (VineDresser) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/2879/literature-notes-vs-permanent-notes) vs Literature notes need not be concise or in your own words; excerpts and key phrases are fine (JasperMcFly) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/20228#Comment_20228)
- Raw highlights/reference notes need not be kept once refactored into own-language notes (Will) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/11804#Comment_11804) vs Keep extensive literal quotes next to your own words; the author's words are the reference (harr) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/21091#Comment_21091)
- Write the zettel without looking at the original text, to avoid using it as a crutch (GeoEng51) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/13136#Comment_13136) vs Write your restatement right next to the quote; easier with the original in view (harr) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/21091#Comment_21091)
- A literature note is a reference in a reference manager and never goes into the slip-box (ZettelDistraction) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/14269#Comment_14269) vs Every source gets its own literature note inside the ZK with reading notes (erikh) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/16300#Comment_16300)
- Keep literature notes separate so they don't dilute the slip-box (Nick) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/12619#Comment_12619) vs Don't keep literature and permanent notes in separate apps; search gets impoverished (Will) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/10497#Comment_10497)
- Enter rough reading notes whole into the ZK and edit in place; no separate literature-note collection (Will) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/12633#Comment_12633) vs Rough reading notes stay outside the ZK; including them is not useful (GeoEng51) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/12632#Comment_12632)
- Don't keep literature notes once processed; v1 is gone after refactoring (Will) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/12633#Comment_12633) vs Strongly advise keeping literature notes; they inspire different zettels over time (Anna_Eleuteri) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/19575#Comment_19575)
- Don't mark notes as yours vs someone else's; just cite in each note (Andy) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/17423#Comment_17423) vs Keep a visible signal (title format) distinguishing others' words from your own syntheses (davecan) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/11052#Comment_11052)
- Any note containing a quotation remains a literature note even with your comments (AvidReader) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/26565#Comment_26565) vs A note that starts as a pure quotation becomes your own as you add comments, and lives with regular notes (msteffens) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/24981#Comment_24981)
- An LLM can draft literature notes that you lightly edit (Edmund) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/18557#Comment_18557) vs The ZK should hold only content from verifiable sources, not LLM output (harr) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25054#Comment_25054)
- Zettelize sources just-in-time, only when a project needs them (Eurobubba) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/2519#Comment_2519) vs You can't know relevance in advance, which is why you process into the ZK (Will) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/2362#Comment_2362)
- Atomic notes are about concepts and ideas, not facts; facts stay in the source or literature note (JasperMcFly) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/25194#Comment_25194) vs Permanent notes can be facts, definitions, or concept descriptions (andang76) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/19301#Comment_19301)
- Literature notes are atomic per reference: one per source (zk_1000) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/17374#Comment_17374) vs A literature note captures a single idea from a single source (davecan) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/11284#Comment_11284)
- Skip literature notes; put references directly in each zettel (GeoEng51) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/26502#Comment_26502) vs Keep every source as its own literature note (erikh) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/16300#Comment_16300)
- If the author said it best and you have nothing to add, quote directly (taurusnoises) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/13146#Comment_13146) vs Store only concepts reframed in your own words; throw away first-pass notes (micahredding) (https://forum.zettelkasten.de/discussion/comment/1927#Comment_1927)

## Coverage log

Method for every row: Vanilla forum API v2 on forum.zettelkasten.de. Discovery used `GET /api/v2/search?query=<q>&recordTypes=discussion`, then `GET /api/v2/discussions/{id}` for countComments and countViews. Threads were read through `GET /api/v2/discussions/{id}` (opening post) and `GET /api/v2/comments?discussionID={id}&expand=insertUser` (replies, with comment permalinks). `GET /api/v2/discussions?sort=-countComments` failed with HTTP 422 (sort accepts only dateLastComment, dateInserted, discussionID, score, hot), so ranking was done locally on the counts. `GET /api/v2/discussions/search?query=<q>` returned counts but only 10 results per query regardless of `limit` and `page`, so it was not used for discovery. Forum handles `Sascha` and `ctietze` were treated as staff (the two authors named at https://zettelkasten.de/authors/).

## Known gaps

Saturation points per topic (3 consecutive on-topic threads adding no new claim):

- atomicity: list exhausted after 37 on-topic threads (31 from cands.json + 6 from extra searches: 3004, 2908, 3109, 1535, 2085, 882); no run of 3 consecutive no-new-claim threads. Closest: 2501 (0 new), 3327 (0), 359 (1); 3402 (0), 3399 (0), then 3004 (18). Novelty judged per thread against all claims from earlier-ranked threads.
- length: list exhausted after 7 on-topic threads (1651, 1107, 302, 1127, 2001, 796, plus extra-search 3109); no saturation, only 796 added nothing (no non-staff claims).
- title: stopped after thread 1864 (How to tie the title with the note's idea), threads 331, 1441, 1864 added nothing new (last new claim at 2508, rank 64; ranks 89-91 are off-topic tooling, not read). Extra search hits 1709, 2919, 3260 read after the list walk.
- good-zettel: cands.json list exhausted after 7 on-topic threads (2837, 1345, 2112, 468, 3399, 2932, 2845) with no saturation. Continued into the Critique my Zettel category (categoryID 17, 53 discussions, ranked by countComments): stopped after thread 2245 (Trajectory of Acquisition), threads 2111, 2227, 2245 added nothing new. Extra "good note" search hits 1421 and 900 read after.
- linking: list exhausted after 29 on-topic threads; never reached 3 consecutive no-new threads. Last run: 1763 (new), 3429 (new), 912 (new), 3423 (none), 1112 (none). The search pool was not exhausted: the "backlinks" and "connection" searches returned about 100 more titles not walked, e.g. 3529, 3346, 3216, 2768, 1027, 1045 by thread ID.
- link-context: the criterion was met after thread 1763 (How do you link/navigate your notes?). Threads 787, 1333, 1763 added no new link-context claim. Walking on, 3429 (What and Where is a "Link Context"?) still added one (attribute the source in the sentence). After it, 912, 3423, 1112 added none. So saturation is weak at the tail, not firm.
- structure-note: stopped after thread 2955 (ZK101 Structure Note example – Purity), threads 2431, 2844, 2955 added nothing new (3220 also nothing new). Rule was first met earlier, after thread 2768 (3575, 3027, 2768 added nothing new); I kept reading past it and later threads (3178, 2910, 3221, 3491, 3242, 2660) did add new claims, so the early trigger was premature.
- id-folgezettel: stopped after thread 2264 (Zettelkasting Eufriction), threads 2247, 639, 2264 added nothing new. Thread 187 (Zettel ID) was read out of rank order after the stop and added two minor claims (ID at end of file name, seconds in timestamps).
- own-words: list exhausted after 29 on-topic threads (52 candidates; last on-topic 633 added nothing, 2976 before it added new). Extra searches ("rephrase", "restate", "reformulate", "quotes") added 1189, 1719, 2479, 2129, 66, 3332; still not saturated (run 1719/2129/66 had no new claims but was broken by 1189, 2479, and finally 3332 which added new). Stopped there for budget.
- literature-note: stopped after thread 2977 (Folgezettel for reference note?), threads 1971, 2984, 2977 added nothing new. Method note: threads already read in the own-words walk (2119, 1747, 3396, 2983, 2879, 2130, 671, 1807, 2501, 2976) were treated as neutral in the literature-note count (neither reset nor counted). 3423 and 1733 were glanced at after saturation (no claims). 935 (extra search hit) read after saturation and added 2 claims.

Other gaps:

- extra searches 'zettel too long' and 'split note' returned 0 hits (forum full-text search seems to AND terms); I widened to 'too long', 'split', 'long notes', 'one idea', 'one thought', 'granularity', 'atomic', 'note size', 'note length' and read the on-topic new hits (3004, 2908, 3109, 1535, 2085, 882). Other new hits judged off-topic by title only and not recorded in coverage (e.g. 3465, 2588, 1655, 1820, 3319); 2581 '[Zettel Feedback] Meta zettelkasting note review' not read.
- walk order put extra-search threads after the whole cands list rather than interleaving by comment count.
- 2085 ZettelDistraction rows quote text inside a blockquote that he introduces as an excerpt from his own Zettel template (his words, not someone else's); kept. Drop those rows if all blockquoted text must go.
- 2570 post by ceaf has body '...' in the dump; no claims possible.
- many claims are off the atomicity/length topics (titles, linking, literature notes, person notes, problem-solution notes) because whole threads were mined; topics column tags them.
- Long borderline threads (881, 1729, 1726, 2919) were read only for title-related passages plus a few posts, so non-title claims in them may be missed (other shards may cover them).
- Long threads 2837, 1345, 2116, 3401, 3109, 2906, 2581 were read with each post truncated (about 400-1300 chars); claims late in long posts may be missed. Quoted GPT output in 2837/2932 was not treated as poster claims.
- Critique category threads below 2245 in the comment ranking (2245 onward: 2497, 3021, 2107, 2737 read; 2259, 2241, 2230, 2240, 2560, 2844, 2495, 2428, 2955, 2218, 3222, 2106, 2524, 2462, 2503, 2954, 2437, 2446, 2434) not read after saturation. Many have staff OPs.
- Search hits not in cands and not read: 3020, 3193, 3279, 3255, 3182 (title search), 2353 (good note search), 2906-adjacent 2843. Title search returns recency-ordered full-text hits, mostly off-topic.
- Some claims duplicate across authors (e.g. titles-as-API, verb vs noun phrases, single focus) were recorded once, so a claim's row count understates support.
- Did not walk the full results of the "backlinks", "connection", and "linking" searches (about 150 unique titles). I picked about 20 by on-topic titles. Weekly "what's happening" threads and tool threads in those results were not considered.
- Long threads (1478, 993, 3515) were read with posts cut at about 800 to 1100 characters, plus targeted full reads where a post was cut off mid-claim. A claim deep inside a very long post may have been missed.
- GeoEng51 seems to contradict himself across years: "make all links connect both ways" in 2020 (comment 8919) vs "only when I think the link should go both ways" in 2024 (comment 19648). Both are recorded. This is not listed as a conflict since it is one poster.
- Long threads (1982, 978, 2125, 2163, 3425, 3529, 1045, 61, 1543) were read with each post truncated to 450-700 characters; blockquoted and indented-quote lines were dropped. Claims deep inside long posts may be missed. Full posts were pulled only where a truncated post looked claim-bearing.
- Staff (Sascha, ctietze) replies were shown only as 300-character context and never recorded; System OP bodies skipped.
- Extra searches ("hub note", "index note", "entry point") surfaced threads outside cands.json. Read: 1295, 161, 99, 1070, 2755, 1543, 61, 209 (skipped), 1762, 2819. Dumped but not read: 1102 (Finally, Folgezettel), 1027 (My attempt to solve the Folgezettel debate). Not dumped: 3319, 3090, 2534, 2158, 1965, 1452, 1264, 3557, 3212, 3148, 1668, 1122, 832, 636.
- Ranked-list threads after the stop points were not considered (structure-note: 2954, 2382, 3207 among them; id-folgezettel: 326, 1874, 1453 and the rest of the tail).
- Thread 1754 (Really struggling with linking) and 1893 (About tags...again) were skipped for structure-note as belonging to the linking and tags shards; both mention hub notes.
- own-words did not reach saturation; next unread extra-search hits worth checking: 1561 "What kinds of notes do you have in your archive?", 2080 "The Zettelkasten Method for Fiction I", 1149, 2116.
- 3467 (Logseq setup), 143, 53, 247, 438, 2185, 3187 skipped by title without dumping (tool/intro/weekly threads).
- posts truncated at the viewer's 900-2500 char cap were expanded only when the visible part looked on-topic; some long off-topic tails (e.g. tool talk in 2952, 2509) were not read in full.
- staff posts (Sascha, ctietze) read only as 200-char previews; they often carry the substantive answer (e.g. 2984, 2977, 2130), so those threads show read-no-claims.
- Staff posts (Sascha, ctietze) are excluded here and belong to ticket #7. They were read for thread context only.
- Discovery relied on the forum's full-text search, which matches body text. Threads that discuss a topic without using the query words may be missing.
- Members-only areas of zettelkasten.de (Members Area, The Perfect Zettel course) are not public and were not swept.
